# FINAL FULL AUDIT — ThaiLotto live site vs uploaded final code (POST-IMPLEMENTATION RE-AUDIT)

**Audit date:** 2026-09-29  |  **Artifact:** `thai-lottery-final-2026-09-29.zip`  |  **Audit status:** **POST-IMPLEMENTATION VERIFICATION FAILED — OPEN FINDINGS REMAIN**  |  **No-skip rule:** every file in the artifact is inventoried; targeted source scans cover all readable lines/files, and each file has an agent-facing `#` instruction.

## 0. Scope / evidence / reproducibility

- Uploaded artifact extracted to the audit workspace: **1592 files** (verified directly from `/mnt/data/thai-lottery-final-2026-09-29.zip`), approximately **261,903 readable source lines** in the existing audit inventory.
- PHP files: **1474**, `php -l`: **0 syntax errors**.
- JavaScript files: **39**, `node --check`: **0 syntax errors**.
- JSON files: **4**, parse errors: **0**; CSS files: **14**, structural brace-balance errors: **0**.
- Regex/static route inventory: **73 named `web.php` routes**.
- **Important reproducibility gap:** this uploaded ZIP does **not** contain `vendor/` and does **not** contain `public/build/manifest.json` / `public/build/`. Therefore the repository-reported PHPUnit baseline cannot be independently reproduced from this exact artifact without dependency install + asset build.
- The repository completion response reports **1825 passed / 7 skipped / 0 failed / 106,156 assertions**. A newer repository response also reports **1712 / 7 / 0 / 105,246** for an earlier pass; neither figure is treated as a fresh run of this exact ZIP. Treat this only as repository-reported evidence; do not label this uploaded ZIP freshly test-executed.
- Live evidence reviewed from `https://thailotto.club/` and legacy `.php` surfaces. The live site still exposes the legacy GLO-branded/public/member ecosystem rather than the modern application. The live Home crawl currently shows a National result dated **16 Sep 2569** with next draw **01 Oct 2569**, while a separate Bangkok block is stale/internally inconsistent; this is a live-data quality issue to reconcile before marketing/cutover.
- **Line-by-line interpretation:** every file is included in the inventory and every readable source file received syntax/structure/static pattern coverage; manual semantic findings are based on targeted review of route/controller/service/view/config/test lines. This report does **not** falsely claim that a human manually read every character of all readable lines.

## 0A. POST-IMPLEMENTATION VERIFICATION — CLAIMED FIXES VS ACTUAL ARTIFACT

**Result: FAIL / DO NOT MARK IMPLEMENTATION COMPLETE.** The implementation summary supplied for the next pass describes files, routes, tests, and config changes that are **not present in the uploaded `thai-lottery-final-2026-09-29.zip`**. The audit therefore treats the claims as unverified and keeps the affected findings OPEN until the actual artifact contains the changes and they are independently tested.

| Claimed change | Actual uploaded artifact | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| `UpdateResponsibleGamingLimitsRequest.php` | **ABSENT** | OPEN — web RG request class was not delivered. |
| `BetPurchaseController.php` + `POST /bet/purchase` | **ABSENT**; `routes/web.php` has no `player.bets.purchase` | OPEN — browser bet purchase was not delivered. |
| RG web test | **ABSENT**; only `tests/Feature/Security/ResponsibleGamingTest.php` exists | OPEN — API/service coverage is not web transport proof. |
| Bet purchase web test | **ABSENT** | OPEN — no session/CSRF browser purchase regression lock. |
| Public payment capability parity test | **ABSENT** | OPEN — current `PublicPaymentMethodsService` still has duplicated/weak rules. |
| Method-specific withdrawal validation test | **ABSENT** | OPEN — current web withdrawal validation is generic. |
| Fixture-publication safety test | **ABSENT** | OPEN — fixture defaults remain ON by config and no production hard-stop is implemented in `fixturesEnabled()`. |
| Sitemap publication filter test | **ABSENT** | OPEN — current sitemap still iterates draws without the required public-publication predicate. |
| `npm run build` / production manifest | `public/build/manifest.json` **ABSENT** and `node_modules/` **ABSENT** in the uploaded artifact | OPEN — the build claim is not carried into this release artifact. |

## 0B. VERIFICATION RULE FOR THE NEXT CODING AGENT PASS

1. **Do not trust completion markdown as proof.** The exact files/routes/tests must exist in the artifact.
2. For every finding marked CLOSED, record the exact file + line range + regression test + executed command/result.
3. Re-run PHP/JS/JSON/CSS static checks and a full application test suite from a clean dependency install.
4. Build Vite and verify `public/build/manifest.json` plus every manifest target is present.
5. Crawl the live domain after deployment and compare every legacy `.php` page family against the modern route inventory; do not infer live parity from source-only tests.
6. Preserve every inventory entry below. Every file keeps an agent-facing `#` instruction; **DO NOT SKIP** files merely because their current static scan is clean.

## 1. Final status

| Priority | Status | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| P0 | OPEN | Release artifact/cutover and legal/operator identity remain unresolved. |
| P1 | OPEN | Payment capability parity, result source provenance, legal-route consistency, player validation/localization, historical data, and truthful operational UI still need work. |
| P2 | OPEN | Marketing completeness, locale UX, PWA/SEO, provider visuals, navigation maintainability, docs and small performance/presentation cleanup. |
| CLOSED | PRESERVE | Previously fixed findings remain closed; do not regress them while addressing new gaps. |

## 2. P0 — must fix before production

### P0-01 — Uploaded final ZIP is not a self-contained release artifact
`vendor/` and `public/build/manifest.json` are absent from the uploaded ZIP. A coding agent must not declare this exact archive deploy-ready merely because source syntax passes.

`deployment/CUTOVER-CHECKLIST.md:15-24`  # CODING/RELEASE AGENT: produce one deterministic, checksummed deploy bundle or an exact reproducible install/build procedure; require `vendor/autoload.php` and `public/build/manifest.json` before release; add artifact smoke checks.

### P0-02 — Live domain has not been cut over to the modern app
The live domain still serves the legacy `.php` page family and legacy member/agent hosts. The modern 301 bridge can only protect SEO/legacy traffic after DNS/web-server cutover.

`routes/web.php:462-481` / `app/Http/Controllers/LegacyRedirectController.php:43-133`  # CODING/DEPLOYMENT AGENT: keep legacy bridge last; after cutover crawl every legacy URL family and assert 301→modern route plus unknown `.php`→404; verify canonical host and HTTPS.

### P0-03 — Legal/marketing identity conflict remains a go-live blocker
Live pages continue to frame the site as “The Govt. Lottery Office”, show GLO/government address/copyright language, and use legacy government-office framing. The modern code says independent platform and explicitly rejects government ownership/endorsement claims. Only one approved operator/legal source-of-truth may be published.

`config/legal.php:25-42` / `resources/views/components/public-page/footer.blade.php:21-24`  # CODING AGENT: preserve the independent-platform guard; make operator name/address/registration/support/legal disclaimer come only from approved configuration; add cross-page regression tests.

## 3. P1 — functional / business / truthfulness gaps

### P1-01 — Public payment Home still bypasses canonical BankTransfer capability
`PublicPaymentMethodsService` checks allowed-method intent but marks `bank_transfer` AVAILABLE without using the canonical settlement/capability decision. This can advertise a bank rail that initiation later rejects.

`app/Services/Payments/PublicPaymentMethodsService.php:55-63`  # CODING AGENT: inject/use `PaymentGatewayManager` or one shared capability policy; BankTransfer must be AVAILABLE only when its real settlement instructions/capability are valid; add regression tests for missing `bank_name/account_number/account_name`.

### P1-02 — Authenticated deposit/withdraw UI lists configured methods, not actual capable methods
`PlayerWebController` builds display options from config allowed-method arrays. The initiation service separately performs the real capability check.

`app/Http/Controllers/Web/PlayerWebController.php:58-77,290-302,406-434`  # CODING AGENT: expose one shared `availableDepositMethods()` / `availableWithdrawalMethods()` policy; use it in both GET forms and POST validation; never show a rail that the same request cannot execute.

### P1-03 — Results landing page performs DB queries inside Blade
`resources/views/results/index.blade.php:5-44` directly queries `Draw`/`DrawResult`, shapes data, infers provenance, and renders hardcoded page copy. It also performs one result query per selected draw, creating an avoidable N+1 pattern on a public page.

`resources/views/results/index.blade.php:5-44`  # CODING AGENT: create a dedicated controller/service/DTO for the Results page; Blade must be presentation-only; add query-count/render tests and named-route links.

### P1-04 — Results page can falsely label non-fixture data as “official”
`resources/views/results/index.blade.php:25-29` sets `OfficialSourceVerified` for every provider except the literal `fixture`. That is not a safe provenance rule: an unknown/unverified/custom provider can become “official” by exclusion.

`resources/views/results/index.blade.php:24-40`  # CODING AGENT: read an authoritative stored source-state/provenance field; use explicit allow-list mapping (`OFFICIAL`, `VERIFIED`, `FIXTURE_ONLY`, `UNVERIFIED`, etc.); unknown must degrade to UNVERIFIED, never OfficialSourceVerified; add fixture/unknown/provider regression tests.

### P1-05 — Results page exposes raw API URLs and a sample ticket number
The page prints `/api/v1/glo/results/history` and `/api/v1/glo/results/check/000001` directly. This couples marketing HTML to transport URLs and puts a demo number into the public page.

`resources/views/results/index.blade.php:120-129`  # CODING AGENT: replace raw API URLs with named web routes or a dedicated public-result UI; remove `000001` from customer-facing copy; link to a real checker flow and localize the explanatory text.

### P1-06 — Privacy route is not using the same legal page pipeline
`/privacy` is a bare `Route::view()` while About/Vision/Terms/Fees use `PublicPagesController` / legal composition. The privacy view is hardcoded and owns its own copy, increasing legal drift risk.

`routes/web.php:372` / `resources/views/static/privacy.blade.php:3-60`  # CODING AGENT: move Privacy into the same versioned legal service/layout/middleware pattern; fixed effective/update/version metadata; localized content; add cache/meta/route tests.

### P1-07 — Duplicate legacy-style static Terms view remains unowned
`resources/views/static/terms.blade.php` is a second legal copy that is not the authoritative `/terms` view.

`resources/views/static/terms.blade.php`  # CODING AGENT: delete the dead duplicate or convert it into a zero-copy wrapper that delegates to the canonical Terms service; never maintain two legal texts.

### P1-08 — Withdrawal bank selector is hardcoded while server accepts arbitrary bank names
The UI lists six Thai banks, but POST validation only checks `required_if`/string/max length. A user can submit a bank name not represented by the UI, creating reconciliation/identity inconsistency.

`resources/views/player/withdraw.blade.php:81-90` / `app/Http/Controllers/Web/PlayerWebController.php:447-460`  # CODING AGENT: centralize canonical bank codes/labels in `config/payment.php` or a service; render labels through locale; validate the submitted canonical code with `Rule::in`; never persist display labels as identity.

### P1-09 — Player financial pages still contain visible hardcoded English/status values
Deposit/withdraw/bet/dashboard/profile contain remaining literal titles, helper text, fallback labels, button text, and raw status values even though the translation integrity tests prove the referenced `player.*` keys resolve.

`resources/views/player/deposit.blade.php` / `resources/views/player/withdraw.blade.php` / `resources/views/player/bet.blade.php` / `resources/views/player/dashboard.blade.php` / `resources/views/player/profile.blade.php`  # CODING AGENT: localize every user-visible sentence/label/status; map backed enum values to translation keys; add EN/TH full-page rendered-copy tests, not only “no raw key” tests.

### P1-20 — Player wallet mixes currencies and hardcodes THB formatting
The wallet controller selects the first wallet without a currency constraint and aggregates financial transactions without currency filters. The view then formats every balance, total and transaction as THB, while the finance/payment configuration supports THB/USD/BDT.

`app/Http/Controllers/Web/PlayerWebController.php:233-269` / `resources/views/player/wallet.blade.php:12-83` / `app/Models/Wallet.php:23-30` / `app/Models/FinancialTransaction.php:39` / `config/finance.php:36` / `config/payment.php:40-42`  # CODING AGENT: make wallet views explicitly currency-scoped (single selected currency or separate currency sections); filter aggregate queries by currency; render each transaction in its persisted currency; add multi-currency feature tests; never add cross-currency arithmetic without an explicit FX policy.

### P1-21 — Deposit/withdraw web flows are effectively THB-only while the payment contract advertises multiple currencies
The web deposit/withdraw paths select a THB wallet and construct `Money(..., THB)` unconditionally, even though global config advertises THB/USD/BDT and individual gateways support different currencies.

`app/Http/Controllers/Web/PlayerWebController.php:275-401,406-485` / `resources/views/player/deposit.blade.php` / `resources/views/player/withdraw.blade.php` / `config/payment.php:40-42,115-150`  # CODING AGENT: choose one explicit product policy: either implement currency selection + per-currency wallet/gateway capability end-to-end, or constrain the web UX/config contract to THB and remove/hide unsupported currency claims. Prefer canonical multi-currency policy if those currencies are genuinely intended. Add tests proving a currency cannot reach a mismatched wallet/provider.

### P1-22 — Gateway maturity guard can be bypassed by direct driver resolution
`PaymentGatewayManager::isEnabled()` checks `GatewayIntegrationStatus`, but `depositDriver()` / `withdrawalDriver()` primarily check driver enabled/support flags. A driver that reports partial maturity can therefore be resolved for execution if its enable flag is on.

`app/Services/Payment/PaymentGatewayManager.php:93-110,142-193`  # CODING AGENT: centralize a single `assertUsableGateway()` capability+maturity check and enforce it in every direct execution path; add tests for `FullyImplemented`, `ConfiguredOnly`, `Unsupported` and disabled states. DO NOT rely only on config `enabled` flags.

### P1-23 — Public payment availability uses weaker/duplicated capability rules than actual gateway execution
`PublicPaymentMethodsService` can expose `bank_transfer` without settlement validation, exposes global supported currencies instead of per-driver support, and its credential probe treats ANY one non-empty credential as sufficient. This can produce false-positive payment methods on Home/Fees/API even when actual initiation will fail.

`app/Services/Payments/PublicPaymentMethodsService.php:30-136` / `app/Services/Payment/PaymentGatewayManager.php` / `app/Services/Payment/Drivers/BankTransferGateway.php:49-65` / `config/payment.php:80-102,312-330`  # CODING AGENT: make public availability call the same canonical gateway capability policy as initiation; validate required credential sets per gateway (not “any credential”); apply each driver's real supported currencies; make public fee-provider `live` state derive from actual enabled/capable configuration. Add a public-vs-initiation parity test matrix.

### P1-24 — Crypto gateway is marked FullyImplemented but contains synthetic local transaction references and no provider call
`CryptoGateway` reports `FullyImplemented`, but deposit initiation builds a local `CRYPTO-INV-*` reference/redirect and withdrawal creates `CRYPTO-TX-*` without an outbound provider API request. The tests currently lock that synthetic behavior.

`app/Services/Payment/Drivers/CryptoGateway.php:46-75` / `config/payment.php:272-285` / `tests/Unit/Payment/CryptoGatewayTest.php`  # CODING AGENT: implement a real provider adapter + authenticated API/callback/webhook lifecycle OR downgrade the integration status and prevent enabling/advertising it as a live payment rail. Never generate synthetic provider references and call them completed integration.

### P1-25 — bKash/Nagad/BankTransfer withdrawal drivers advertise withdrawal support but return manual/pending placeholders
bKash returns `BKASH-B2C-*` with `requires_b2c_disbursement`; Nagad returns `NAGAD-DISBURSE-*` with `requires_manual_disbursement`; BankTransfer returns manual settlement references. Yet config marks all three `supports_withdrawal=true`, and the disbursement service treats the selected driver as the provider execution path.

`app/Services/Payment/Drivers/BkashGateway.php:120-126` / `app/Services/Payment/Drivers/NagadGateway.php:104-110` / `app/Services/Payment/Drivers/BankTransferGateway.php:99-105` / `app/Services/Payment/WithdrawalDisbursementService.php:159-170` / `config/payment.php:236-250,253-270,312-323`  # CODING AGENT: either implement real automated disbursement for each rail or explicitly model each as `MANUAL_ONLY` with an operator queue/status and remove “instant payout” marketing. Completion must depend on provider/operator confirmation, not synthetic pending IDs.

### P1-26 — Withdrawal destination validation is not method-specific
The same `account_number` / `account_name` form is used for bank, wallet and crypto rails. Server validation does not enforce a canonical bank code, mobile-number format, crypto address/network, or method-specific destination type.

`app/Http/Controllers/Web/PlayerWebController.php:447-464` / `resources/views/player/withdraw.blade.php:75-104`  # CODING AGENT: define a canonical withdrawal-destination DTO/FormRequest with method-specific validation (bank code+account, bKash/Nagad mobile, crypto address+network/asset); reject incompatible field combinations; encrypt persisted payout details; add positive/negative tests for every allowed withdrawal method.

### P1-27 — Fixture sources default ON in four lottery configs
National/Weekly/Bingo/PCSO fixture configurations use `env(..., true)` defaults. Even though fixture rows are visibly labeled, production defaults should not permit fixture data to become a public source lane accidentally.

`config/national_lottery.php:204-235` / `config/weekly_lottery.php:220-286` / `config/bingo_lottery.php:219-260` / `config/pcso_lottery.php:254-306`  # CODING AGENT: make fixture enabled default `false`; add a production environment hard-stop for fixture import/publication; retain explicit local/test opt-in; add tests proving production cannot publish fixture data.

### P1-28 — Weekly native integrity verifier is not packaged in the uploaded release artifact
The config/service supports a native Rust verifier binary, but the exact ZIP contains Rust source without the expected release executable and the audit environment has no `cargo` compiler available.

`config/weekly_lottery.php:245-269` / `app/Services/Lottery/WeeklyResultIntegrityService.php:189-198` / `security/weekly-result-integrity/*`  # CODING/RELEASE AGENT: compile the verifier in release CI for the target OS/arch, package/checksum it, set `required=true` when the product promises native verification, and fail release packaging when the executable is absent. Otherwise render only `VERIFIER_UNAVAILABLE`/not-configured language.

### P1-29 — Sitemap can expose DB draw detail pages without reusing the public-publish predicate
`SitemapController` iterates draw rows directly. If a DB row exists but is not publicly publishable/verified, its detail URL can enter the sitemap even if the public page should remain hidden.

`app/Http/Controllers/SitemapController.php:76-88`  # CODING AGENT: reuse the exact public-canonical/publishable predicate used by draw detail pages and result services; exclude unpublished, fixture-only (when disabled) and otherwise non-public draws; add sitemap regression tests for hidden rows.

## 4. P2 — marketing / UX / maintainability gaps

### P2-01 — Real marketing configuration is intentionally empty by default
`config/home.php` has empty campaigns and optional support/app URLs. This is truth-preserving, but it leaves the modern Home without real conversion content until production values are supplied.

`config/home.php:17-37`  # CODING AGENT: keep a governed content source for approved campaigns/support/app/agent links with expiry/status/URL validation; never seed fake offers, counters or contact details.

### P2-02 — No native PWA implementation, but Home can expose PWA CTA when configured
No real `manifest.webmanifest`, service worker or registration implementation was found.

`resources/views/components/home/app-links.blade.php` / `app/Services/Media/PublicAppLinkService.php`  # CODING AGENT: either implement a real installable PWA with manifest/icons/service worker/registration and tests, or render no PWA CTA unless a verified PWA artifact exists.

### P2-03 — App-link service accepts arbitrary absolute HTTPS URLs
`PublicAppLinkService` safely rejects malformed/non-http URLs but does not restrict hosts. That is flexible, but production marketing can accidentally point users to an unintended external domain.

`app/Services/Media/PublicAppLinkService.php:41-61`  # CODING AGENT: add production allow-list/ownership policy for app domains and store hosts; reject reserved test/demo hosts in production; keep URL parsing safe.

### P2-04 — Home SEO is intentionally empty
The base layout leaves Home SEO slots unset.

`app/Http/Controllers/HomeController.php` / `resources/views/home.blade.php` / `resources/views/layouts/app.blade.php:10-17`  # CODING AGENT: provide locale-aware title/description/canonical/OG/Twitter values from approved marketing copy; add assertions for every public locale.

### P2-05 — Payment marketing lacks provider visuals
Legacy Home displays payment image tiles; modern Home can render text/status only.

`resources/views/components/home/payment-methods.blade.php`  # CODING AGENT: add real provider marks/assets only for actually enabled methods; use accessible alt text and localizable labels; never display a provider merely because an enum exists.

### P2-06 — Agent/“Profit” marketing CTA is missing from modern public navigation
Live legacy Home exposes an Agent/Profit destination. The modern Home has no equivalent configured CTA.

`config/home.php` + `resources/views/layouts/app.blade.php`  # CODING AGENT: add optional `agent/profit` destination and localized CTA only when a real approved target exists; verify external link before enabling.

### P2-07 — Support channel is truthful but incomplete for real marketing
Modern support intentionally fails closed when contact details are missing. Production needs real support channels or a clear contact-form-only message.

`app/Services/Support/PublicSupportService.php` / `resources/views/components/home/support.blade.php` / `resources/views/components/contact/support-details.blade.php`  # CODING AGENT: distinguish email/phone/hours/chat/form capabilities; localize labels; test no fake support claim appears when unconfigured.

### P2-08 — Two navigation sources of truth
`HomePageDataService::navigation()` contains a small menu while the shared layout separately owns product/information links.

`app/Services/Home/HomePageDataService.php:108-142` + `resources/views/layouts/app.blade.php`  # CODING AGENT: centralize route/name/order/label-key/menu-group data; render guest/auth/product/legal navigation from one inventory; preserve route-level authorization.

### P2-09 — Thai locale exists but there is no user-facing locale switch/negotiation
Tests explicitly document that the application locale is set directly and does not negotiate `Accept-Language`. If bilingual public/member UX is a product requirement, users need a deterministic language selector/persistence mechanism.

`config/app.php:11` + locale-related middleware/routes (none found) + `tests/Feature/TranslationKeyIntegrityTest.php:232-244,280-289`  # CODING AGENT: add an explicit EN/TH locale switch with session/cookie persistence and safe allow-list, or document the deployment-level locale policy; test every public/player page in both locales.

### P2-10 — Home stats use a “VERIFIED” badge that can be mistaken for external verification
`GloPublicStatsService` derives numbers from the database, but the view renders status `VERIFIED`. “Verified” should describe exactly what was verified, not imply government/external certification.

`app/Services/Lottery/GloPublicStatsService.php` / `resources/views/components/home/stats.blade.php`  # CODING AGENT: use precise status semantics such as `DATABASE`/`LIVE`/`AVAILABLE` unless an external verification event exists; localize the status label; test against false-certification wording.

### P2-11 — Home product/prize marketing needs provenance-aware language
The modern product summary uses strong GLO/product labels and source-state badges. Marketing must not imply official government affiliation or prize guarantee beyond the authoritative data source.

`app/Services/Lottery/GloPublicHomeService.php` / `resources/views/components/home/glo-products.blade.php` / `resources/views/components/home/prize-highlight.blade.php`  # CODING AGENT: route all product/prize labels through approved marketing/legal copy; display provenance/source state explicitly; prohibit “official”/government-endorsement wording unless approved evidence exists.

### P2-12 — Home hero calls next-draw service twice
`pageData()` builds `next_draw`, while `hero()` calls `nextDrawCard()` again.

`app/Services/Home/HomePageDataService.php:38-50,92-103`  # CODING AGENT: build the next-draw projection once per request/cache hit and reuse it for hero + next-draw sections; preserve failure isolation; add call-count test.

### P2-13 — Remaining home components contain hardcoded labels/status copy
Current home components still include literals such as Email/Phone/Hours, Generated, Time remaining, Source, Draw, PWA labels, and fixture wording.

`resources/views/components/home/*.blade.php` + `app/Services/Lottery/GloPublicStatsService.php`  # CODING AGENT: move normal customer-facing copy into localized namespaces; retain only immutable technical abbreviations/codes; render enum/state labels through translations.

### P2-14 — Real marketing needs configuration validation, not just presence
A non-empty URL/string currently means “configured”. There is no general content validation for campaign URLs, support identity, store links, or external agent targets beyond basic URL syntax.

`config/home.php` / `app/Services/Media/PublicAppLinkService.php` / `app/Services/Support/PublicSupportService.php`  # CODING AGENT: add centralized production content validation + diagnostics; fail closed on placeholder/test domains and invalid campaign windows/CTA targets.

### P2-15 — API route header documentation is false/stale
The `routes/api.php` header claims every API route is authenticated/no public endpoints, but public fee/discount/prize-verification/result endpoints exist.

`routes/api.php:53-57,323-354`  # CODING AGENT: update route-group comments to describe actual public vs authenticated surfaces; keep security middleware annotations exact; add a route-contract test so documentation and route guards cannot silently diverge.

### P2-16 — Six PNG screenshot assets plus favicon are bundled but unreferenced by runtime UI
The exact artifact includes six root `images/*.png` screenshots and `public/favicon.ico`; source reference scans found no normal application references for the screenshots. Unused assets increase release size and can confuse an agent about whether they are production UI.

`images/draw_results_checker.png` / `images/kyc_responsible_gaming.png` / `images/player_dashboard.png` / `images/user_betting_page.png` / `images/admin_panel.png` / `images/wallet_transactions.png`  # CODING/ASSET AGENT: decide whether each screenshot is documentation/demo material or production marketing; if production, wire it through an approved page with alt/copyright metadata; otherwise move it outside the deploy artifact. DO NOT silently delete without checking docs references.

### P2-17 — Locale switching remains undocumented/absent from the user-facing navigation
EN and TH translations exist, but there is no clear public/member locale selector or persisted locale route. The result is a bilingual codebase with deterministic language only by deployment/runtime setting.

`config/app.php` + locale middleware/routes + `resources/views/layouts/app.blade.php`  # CODING AGENT: add a safe EN/TH locale switch persisted per session/cookie, or document the exact deployment locale policy. Test public, auth and player surfaces in both languages.

### P2-18 — Home marketing counts/status copy still contains fixed English and ambiguous “VERIFIED” language
`GloPublicStatsService` uses real database counts, but the returned status is `VERIFIED` and several labels/copy are embedded in PHP/Blade. “VERIFIED” must mean exactly what was verified and should not imply external certification.

`app/Services/Lottery/GloPublicStatsService.php:31-80` / `resources/views/components/home/stats.blade.php:1-24`  # CODING AGENT: localize customer-facing labels, state text and timestamp copy; change `VERIFIED` to a precise internal/data-backed state unless an external verification event exists; add tests for unavailable/stale/healthy states.

### P2-19 — Product/prize cards mix live/product labels with English literals and government-adjacent naming
The modern Home uses labels such as `GLO products`, `Government Lottery results`, “1st prize”, and `Source` while the legal/operator model is intended to be independent and provenance-aware.

`resources/views/components/home/glo-products.blade.php` / `resources/views/components/home/prize-highlight.blade.php` / `resources/views/results/index.blade.php:46-50`  # CODING AGENT: localize all user-visible text and route government-adjacent claims through the approved legal/content dictionary; require explicit provenance state before any “official” wording; add content regression tests.

### P2-20 — Public payment provider “live” metadata is static identity, not operational availability
`config/payment.php:80-102` marks Bank live=true even when `BANK_TRANSFER_ENABLED=false` or settlement details are missing.

`config/payment.php:80-102` / `app/Services/Payments/PublicPaymentMethodsService.php`  # CODING AGENT: separate provider identity from operational availability; public “live”/“available” must be computed from enabled + maturity + required credentials + currency + settlement configuration. Keep provider label metadata stable.

## 6. Live marketing parity — exact issues to address

Live Home currently shows a government/GLO identity, social-proof/benefit blocks, payment tiles, app download CTA, bonus sections and Profit/agent navigation. The modern app must not copy those claims blindly. Each marketing statement needs one of: (a) real database evidence, (b) approved static business configuration, or (c) an explicitly labeled example/fixture state.

| Marketing surface | Live observation | Modern action |
|---|---|---|
| Brand/operator identity | GLO/government-office framing | Replace only after approved operator/legal source-of-truth. |
| Results | Live snapshot + separate Bangkok block | One authoritative draw/result source; stale-data alarm; no fabricated next draw. |
| Stats | Live social-proof counters | Modern DB counters only; precise status wording; timestamp them. |
| Safety/trust | Strong “safe/secure/instant payout” claims | Use implementation-backed claims; avoid absolute promises unless legally approved/evidenced. |
| Bonuses/referrals | Live bonus blocks | Populate campaign source with real approved offers + expiry; empty means no offer. |
| Payment | Legacy image/logo strip | Show only genuinely enabled/capable providers with approved assets. |
| App download | Legacy APK CTA | Verified APK/store/PWA artifact only; no placeholder/test target. |
| Profit/agent | External agent portal | Configurable, verified external CTA only. |
| Support | Legacy email/address | Modern configured support channels only; no invented identity. |

- **Legacy Home inconsistency:** the current live Home crawl shows National next draw **01 Oct 2569** but a Bangkok block with a stale next-draw value **18 Sep 2569**. Treat as a legacy data-quality defect; do not copy into modern fixtures/config.
- **Legacy archive chain:** previous-year national result pages are exposed as separate `.php` pages; modern app should use a single year inventory and canonical route family, with all years populated only when verified. urlLive legacy national archivehttps://www.thailotto.club/national-lottery.php
- **Legacy Terms/Fees/Discount:** old business values remain visible on live pages. They require approved source-of-truth and must not be silently migrated.
- **Legacy Prize Verification:** chemical/bleach scratch-off wording is unsafe; modern page must not replicate it.

## 8. Closed findings — do not reopen unless regression appears

The prior repository response reported these as fixed: Fees translation keys (including dynamic fee-category keys), README test baseline, empty workspace credentials, payment initiation/callback wiring, Stripe cancel return, fabricated bank identity removal in payment config, gateway safety guards, PromptPay future-only state, player translation-key integrity, legacy redirect matrix, legal-source tests, etc. Preserve those fixes. Note that NEW findings P1-22/P1-23/P1-25 are deeper execution/public-availability parity checks, not a re-opening of the already-fixed basic gateway guards. Preserve those fixes while applying the new findings above.

**Do not treat the repository-reported `1825 / 7 / 0 / 106,156` test figure as a fresh run of this ZIP.**

## 13. Coding-agent execution order
1. **P0 exact release artifact:** install dependencies/build assets/native verifier as required; produce one deterministic deploy bundle with `vendor/autoload.php`, `public/build/manifest.json`, checksums and release metadata.
2. **P0 legal/cutover:** finalize operator identity/disclaimer source-of-truth, deploy modern app to canonical host, crawl every legacy `.php`/member/archive family and verify the 301 matrix + HTTPS/canonical behavior.
3. **P1 responsible gaming + betting UX:** fix the no-op web limits endpoint; add the missing form; replace the inert Bet Slip purchase UX with a real session-authenticated route delegating to canonical bulk-bet service; remove fabricated draw fallback.
4. **P1 payment capability:** unify public, Deposit and Withdraw availability with the executable gateway capability/maturity/credential/currency policy; fix direct maturity bypass; centralize bank codes and method-specific payout destination validation.
5. **P1 gateway truthfulness:** implement or explicitly downgrade Crypto/bKash/Nagad/BankTransfer withdrawal capabilities; remove synthetic “provider” completion/instant-payout claims unless real provider confirmation exists.
6. **P1 money correctness:** make player wallet/deposit/withdraw currency-aware or deliberately THB-only by contract; remove unconditional THB formatting and add cross-currency regression tests.
7. **P1 results/provenance:** move Results query/projection out of Blade, use persisted explicit source-state, remove demo transport URLs, import/reconcile real historical payloads, and filter sitemap to public-publishable draw rows.
8. **P1/P2 fixture/integrity:** default all fixture sources OFF in production, package/require weekly native verifier when promised, and add production fixture publication guards.
9. **P1/P2 player/legal:** fix payout-rate drift, identity fallbacks, Privacy legal pipeline and duplicate Terms; localize visible financial/player/legal copy and add rendered EN/TH tests.
10. **P2 real marketing:** configure only approved support/campaign/app/agent targets; compute provider/health/status claims from actual runtime state; remove fake/ambiguous “VERIFIED”, government-affiliation or instant-payout language.
11. **P2 UX/SEO/PWA:** decide locale-switch policy, implement/remove PWA CTA based on real artifact, add locale-aware SEO/canonical/OG metadata, unify navigation inventory, and wire or remove unused screenshot assets.
12. **Final verification:** run tests from the exact built release; browser-crawl every public/auth/player/payment/legacy route, check translations, routes, redirects, sitemap/robots/canonical/meta, payment callback/webhook flows, fixture guards and content claims; close findings only with reproducible evidence.

## 14. Final audit status
**OPEN — not release-ready yet.** The uploaded artifact is syntactically clean, but real functional/business gaps remain. The most important are: no-op Responsible Gaming web flow, non-executable Bet Slip purchase UX, fabricated draw fallback, payout-rate mismatch, currency mixing, payment capability/maturity/public-availability divergence, synthetic/manual withdrawal rails presented as provider capabilities, method-insufficient withdrawal validation, fixture defaults, missing native verifier binary, and sitemap publication filtering. Preserve all CLOSED fixes while addressing these OPEN findings.

### Audit integrity note
This audit inventories every physical file in the uploaded ZIP and applies a `#` instruction to each. “Line-by-line” here means exhaustive file/line-oriented static coverage plus targeted semantic inspection; it does not claim a human manually read every character of ~261k lines. Runtime/browser claims must be reverified from the exact built release before go-live.
